Retrieve + Validate\n        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        auto postOpt = postRepo.findById(postId);\n        if (!postOpt.has_value())\n            throw PostNotFoundError(fmt::format(\"Post id={} not found\", postId));\n\n        domain::Post& post = *postOpt;\n        if (post.status == domain::PostStatus::Published)\n            throw PublishConflictError{fmt::format(\"Post id={} already published\", postId)};\n\n        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 2. Fire cross-cutting concerns outside the transaction\n        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        logAudit(actorUserId, post);\n        enqueueSideEffects(post);\n\n        spdlog::info(\"Post id={} published by actor={}\", postId, actorUserId);\n    }\n\n    void PostPublicationWorkflowService::logAudit(std::string_view actor,\n                                                  const domain::Post& post) const\n    {\n        infra::audit::Event e{\n            .level   = infra::audit::Level::Info,\n            .actor   = std::string{actor},\n            .object  = fmt::format(\"post:{}\", post.id),\n            .action  = \"publish\",\n            .message = fmt::format(\"Post \\\"{}\\\" was published\", post.title)\n        };\n        infra::audit::Logger::instance().log(e);\n    }\n\n    void PostPublicationWorkflowService::enqueueSideEffects(const domain::Post& post) const\n    {\n        auto& dispatcher = infra::jobs::Dispatcher::instance();\n\n        // Full-text index update\n        dispatcher.enqueue<jobs::ReindexPostJob>(post.id);\n\n        // E-mail notifications (multi-lang)\n        std::string subj = infra::i18n::Translator::translate(\n            \"email.post_published.subject\", post.locale);\n\n        dispatcher.enqueue<jobs::SendPostPublishedEmailJob>(post, subj);\n    }\n} // namespace service\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Unit-style Self-Test (compiled only with ‑DLOCAL_TEST)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#ifdef LOCAL_TEST\n#include <catch2/catch.hpp>\n\nTEST_CASE(\"PostPublicationWorkflowService publishes a draft\")\n{\n    auto session = core::orm::SessionFactory::open();\n    service::PostPublicationWorkflowService svc{session};\n\n    REQUIRE_NOTHROW(svc.publish(42, \"user:123\"));\n}\n#endif\n```","numLines":393,"startLine":1,"totalLines":393}}}

154→public:\n   155→    bool get(const Key& k, Value& out_value) {\n   156→        std::shared_lock lock(mu_);\n   157→        auto it = map_.find(k);\n   158→        if (it == map_.end()) return list_.splice(list_.begin(), list_, it->second);\n   174→            return;\n   175→        }\n   176→        if (list_.size() >= Capacity) {\n   177→            auto last = list_.end();\n   178→            --last;\n   179→            map_.erase(last->first);\n   180→            list_.pop_back();\n   181→        }\n   182→        list_.emplace_front(k, std::move(v));\n   183→        map_[k] = struct Token {\n   216→        TokType     type;\n   217→        std::string text;  // For WORD/PHRASE\n   218→    };\n   219→\n   220→    void tokenize() {\n   221→        static const std::regex 228→            else if (tok == \")\") { tokens_.push_back({TokType::RPAREN, tok}); }\n   229→            else if (icase_eq(tok, \"AND\")) { tokens_.push_back({TokType::AND, tok}); }\n   230→            else if (icase_eq(tok, \"OR\")) { tokens_.push_back({TokType::OR, tok}); }\n   231→            else if (icase_eq(tok, \"NOT\")) { tokens_.push_back({TokType::NOT, tok}); }\n   232→            else {\n\ninline std::string to_lower(std::string str) {\n    std::transform(str.begin(), str.end(), str.begin(),\n                   [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n    return str;\n}\n\ninline std::vector<std::string> split_ws(const std::string& text) {\n    std::vector<std::string> out;\n    std::stringstream ss(text);\n    std::string token;\n    while (ss >> -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntemplate <typename Key, typename Value, std::size_t Capacity = 128>\nclass LruCache {\npublic:\n    bool get(const Key& k, Value& out_value) {\n        std::shared_lock lock(mu_);\n        auto it = map_.find(k);\n        if (it == map_.end()) return false;\n        // Move touched item to front\n        {\n            std::unique_lock ulock(mu_, std::adopt_lock);\n            list_.splice(list_.begin(), list_, it->second);\n        }\n        out_value = it->second->second;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    void put(const Key& k, Value v) {\n        std::unique_lock lock(mu_);\n        auto it = map_.find(k);\n        if (it != map_.end()) {\n            it->second->second = std::move(v);\n            list_.splice(list_.begin(), list_, it->second);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (list_.size() >= Capacity) {\n            auto last = list_.end();\n            --last;\n            map_.erase(last->first);\n            list_.pop_back();\n        }\n        list_.emplace_front(k, std::move(v));\n        map_[k] = list_.begin();\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    mutable std::shared_mutex\n        mu_;  // allows concurrent reads while writes are unique\n    std::list<std::pair<Key, Value>> list_;\n    std::unordered_map<Key, typename std::list<std::pair<Key, Value>>::iterator> -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass QueryParser {\npublic:\n    explicit QueryParser(std::string q)\n        : input_(std::move(q)), pos_(0) {\n        tokenize();\n    }\n\n    std::unique_ptr<QueryNode> parse() {\n        auto node = parse_or();\n        if (pos_token_ != tokens_.size()) {\n            throw ParseError(\"Unexpected token at end of query.\");\n        }\n        return node;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    enum class TokType { WORD, PHRASE, AND, OR, NOT, LPAREN, RPAREN };\n\n    struct Token {\n        TokType     type;\n            std::regex::icase);\n        std::smatch m;\n        std::string s = input_;\n        while (std::regex_search(s, m, re)) {\n            std::string tok = m.str(0);\n            if (tok == \"(\") { tokens_.push_back({TokType::LPAREN, tok}); }\n            else if (tok == \")\") { tokens_.push_back({TokType::RPAREN, tok}); }\n            else if (icase_eq(tok, \"AND\")) { tokens_.push_back({TokType::AND, tok}); }\n            else if (icase_eq(tok, \"OR\")) { tokens_.push_back({TokType::OR, tok}); }\n            else if (icase_eq(tok, \"NOT\")) {                tok.substr(1, tok.size() - 2)});  // strip quotes\n            } else {\n                tokens_.push_back({TokType::WORD, tok});\n            }\n            s = m.suffix();\n        }\n    }\n\n    static bool icase_eq(const std::string& a, const std::string& b) {\n        return util::to_lower(a) == util::to_lower(b);\n    }\n\n    // Recursive-descent parser with operator precedence:\n    //  OR (lowest) -> AND -> NOT -> term/paren (highest)\n    std::unique_ptr<QueryNode> parse_or() {\n        auto left = parse_and();\n        while (match(TokType::OR)) {\n children.push_back(std::move(right));\n            left = QueryNode::make(NodeType::AND, std::move(children));\n        }\n        return left;\n    }\n\n    std::unique_ptr<QueryNode> parse_not() {\n QueryNode::make(NodeType::NOT, std::move(ch));\n        }\n        return parse_term();\n    }\n\n    std::unique_ptr<QueryNode> parse_term() {\n        if (match(TokType::LPAREN)) {\n            auto node = parse_or();\n            if (!match(TokType::RPAREN)) {\n                throw ParseError(\"Expected ')'\");\n            }\n            return node;\n        }\n        if        switch (node->type) {\n            case NodeType::TERM:\n                return match_term(doc, node->term);\n            case NodeType::PHRASE:\n                return match_phrase(doc, node->term);\n            case NodeType::AND:\n                return std::all_of(\n                    node->children.begin(), node->children.end(),\n                    [&](const std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>& ch) { return match_node(ch.get(), doc); });\n            case NodeType::OR:\n                return std::any_of(\n                    node->children.begin(), node->children.end(),\n                    [&](const std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>& ch) { return match_node(ch.get(), doc); });\n            case NodeType::NOT:\n                return !match_node(node->children.front().get(), doc);\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Search Service\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass SearchService {\npublic:\n    explicit SearchService(std::shared_ptr<DocumentRepository> repo,\n                           std::size_t                         cache_entries = 512)\n        : repo_(std::move(repo)), cache_(cache_entries) {}\n\n    // Asynchronous interface: returns future<SearchResponse>\n    std::future<SearchResponse> search_async(SearchRequest req) {\n        return offset/limit at response time to avoid storing slices\n            SearchResponse resp = cached;\n            resp.hits = slice(resp.hits, req.offset, req.limit);\n            resp.took = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n                std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start_time);\n            return resp;\n        }\n\n        // Parse\n        auto parser = QueryParser(req.query);\n        auto ast    = parser.parse();\n\n        // Evaluate\n        auto corpus = repo_->fetch_all();\n        if (!req.locale.empty()) {\n            corpus.erase(std::remove_if(corpus.begin(), corpus.end(),\n                                        [&](const Document& d) {\n                                            return d.locale != req.locale;\n                                        }),\n                         corpus.end());\n        }\n\n        QueryEvaluator evaluator(ast.get());\n        auto           matches = evaluator.filter(corpus);\n\n        // Sort by publish date desc\n        std::sort(matches.begin(), matches.end(), [](const auto& a, const auto& b) {\n            return a.published_at > b.published_at;\n        });\n\n        SearchResponse resp;\n        resp.total_hits = matches.size();\n        resp.hits       = slice(matches, req.offset, req.limit);\n        resp.took       = cache_.put(req.query,\n                   SearchResponse{matches, resp.total_hits,\n                                  resp.took});  // storing took is fine; will be recomputed\n\n        return m_pos);\n            m_pos = end + 1;\n            return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(phrase), negated);\n        }\n\n        // Word\n        std::size_t start = m_pos;\n        while (!eof() && !std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n            ++m_pos;\n\n        std::string word = m_query.substr(start, m_pos - start);\n        return broader system)\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct ArticleDTO\n{\n    std::int64_t             id              {};\n    std::string Extraction\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace ranking {\n\n/**\n *  Very naive TF-IDF style scoring. */\ndouble score_document(std::string_view text,\n                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n{\n    if (search_terms.empty())\n        return 0.0;\n\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> word_freq;\n    std::istringstream iss{std::string(text)};\n    std::string word;\n    while (iss >> word)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(word);\n around the first term hit.\n */\nstd::string make_snippet(std::string_view text,\n                         const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms,\n                         std::size_t context = 40)\n{\n    std::string lowercase{ text };\n    util::ascii_to_lower(lowercase);\n\n    std::size_t best_pos = std::string_view::npos;\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::size_t pos = lowercase.find(term);\n        if (pos != std::string_view::npos &&\n            (best_pos == std::string_view::npos || pos < best_pos))\n            best_pos = pos;\n    }\n\n    if (best_pos == std::string_view::npos)\n        return std::string(text.substr(0, std::min(text.size(), context))) + \"...\";\n\n    std::size_t start = (best_pos > context) ? best_pos - context : 0;\n    std::size_t end   = std::min(text.size(), best_pos + context);\n\n    std::string snippet = std::string(text.substr(start, end - start));\n    // crude HTML highlight\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::regex rgx(term, std::regex_constants::icase);\n        snippet = std::regex_replace(snippet, rgx, \"<mark>$&</mark>\");\n    }\n\n    if (start != 0)\n        snippet.insert(0, \"...\");\n    if (end != text.size())\n        snippet.append(\"...\");\n    return snippet;\n}\n\n} // namespace ranking\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Search Service\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass SearchService\n{\npublic:\n    explicit SearchService(std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository> repo)\n        : m_repo(std::move(repo))\n    {\n        if (!m_repo)\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"SearchService requires a repository\");\n    }\n\n    struct Result\n    {\n        ArticleDTO                      std::size_t offset = 0)\n    {\n        if (query.size() < 2)\n            return {}; // ignore noise\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE\n        query = util::normalize_utf8(query);\n#endif\n\n        // Step 1: Parse\n        std::vector<std::string> bound_params;\n        auto ast  = QueryParser{query}.parse();\n        auto where_clause = ast->to_sql(bound_params);\n\n        // Step 2: Fetch data\n        auto records = m_repo->search(where_clause, bound_params, limit, offset);\n\n        // Step 3: Score + snippet\n sc = ranking::score_document(rec.content, search_terms);\n            std::string snip = ranking::make_snippet(rec.content, search_terms);\n            out.push_back(Result{std::move(rec), sc, std::move(snip)});\n        }\n\n        // Step 4: Sort by score desc, then published_at desc\n        std::ranges::stable_sort(out, [](auto const& a, auto const& b)\n        {\n            if (a.score != b.score)\n                return a.score > b.score;\n            return a.article.published_at > b.article.published_at;\n        });\n\n        spdlog::info(\"Search for '{}' returned {} documents\", query, out.size());\n        return out;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository> m_repo;\n\n    static void collect_terms_recursive(const NodePtr& node,\n                                        std::vector<std::string>& dst)\n    {\n        if (!node)\n            return;\n\n        if (auto term = std::dynamic_pointer_cast<TermNode>(node))\n        {\n            dst.push_back(term->token);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (auto group = std::dynamic_pointer_cast<BoolNode>(node))\n        {\n            for (auto const& ch : group->children)\n                collect_terms_recursive(ch, dst);\n        }\n    }\n\n    static std::vector<std::string> collect_terms(const NodePtr& ast)\n    {\n        std::vector<std::string> terms;\n Translating the AST into a SQL fragment that can be\n *                    embedded safely (with positional parameters) in queries\n *                    issued by our ORM layer.\n *\n *               All public APIs are contained under namespace:\n *                   IntraLedger::Search\n *\n *               The code follows modern C++20 best-practices, makes heavy use\n *               of RAII, read_while(Pred p)\n    {\n        const auto start = m_cursor;\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size() && p(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_input[m_cursor])))\n            ++m_cursor;\n        return std::string { m_input.substr(start, m_cursor - start) };\n    }\n\n    Token lex()\n    {\n        skip_ws();\n        if (m_cursor >= m_input.size())\n            return { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", m_cursor };\n\n        const char ch = m_input[m_cursor];\n\n        // Punctuation tokens\n        switch (ch)\n        {\n            case ':':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::Colon, \":\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '(':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::LParen, \"(\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case ')':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::RParen, \")\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '\"':\n                return lex_phrase();\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n\n        // Word or reserved keyword\n        if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch)) || ch == '_' || ch == '-')\n        {\n            auto word = read_while([](unsigned char c) {\n                return std::isalnum(c) || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '.';\n            });\n\n            const auto lower = to_lower(word);\n            if (lower == \"and\")\n                return { TokenType::And, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"or\")\n                return { TokenType::Or, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"not\")\n                return { TokenType::Not, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n\n            return { TokenType::Word, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { std::string { \"Invalid character '\" } + ch + \"'\", m_cursor };\n    }\n\n    Token lex_phrase()\n    {\n        const std::size_t start_pos = m_cursor;\n        ++m_cursor; // skip opening quote\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        bool escaped = false;\n\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size())\n        {\n            char ch = m_input[m_cursor++];\n            if (escaped)\n            {\n                // Accept common escape sequences or keep char as is\n                switch (ch)\n                {\n                    case '\"':  oss << '\"'; break;\n                    case '\\\\': oss << '\\\\'; break;\n                    case 'n':  oss << '\\n'; break;\n                    case 't':  oss << '\\t'; break;\n                    default:   oss << ch;  break;\n                }\n                escaped = false;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\\\\')\n            {\n                escaped = true;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\"')\n            {\n                // End of phrase\n                return { TokenType::Phrase, oss.str(), start_pos };\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                oss << ch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Reached end-of-input without closing quote\n        throw ParseError { \"Unterminated string literal\", start_pos };\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  AST definitions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct QueryNode;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>;\n\nstruct Term\n{\n    std::string         || std::holds_alternative<Not>(*this);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Parser (recursive-descent)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass Parser\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Parser(std::string_view input) : m_lexer(input)\n    {\n        m_lexer.reset();    // Highest level: OR\n    NodePtr parse_or()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_and();\n        while (match(TokenType::Or))\n        {\n            auto rhs = parse_and();\n            node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Or { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n        }\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Intermediate level: AND (implicit or explicit)\n    NodePtr parse_and()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_unary();\n\n        while (true)\n        {\n            if (match(TokenType::And))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            // Implicit AND: two consecutive terms / groups\n            else if (peek_any({ TokenType::Word, TokenType::Phrase, TokenType::LParen, TokenType::Not }))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Unary: NOT\n    NodePtr parse_unary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::Not))\n        {\n            auto arg = parse_unary();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Not { std::move(arg) });\n        }\n        return               if (!is_whitelisted_field(fieldName))\n                    throw ParseError { \"Unknown filter field '\" + fieldName + \"'\", wordTok.position };\n\n                // Value can be      = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n                {\n                    auto termTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode    = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { termTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    throw auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { \"Unexpected token '\" + m_lexer.peek().lexeme + \"'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    // Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    bool match(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (peek(t))\n        {\n            m_lexer.consume();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    void expect(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (!match(t))\n            throw ParseError { \"Expected        const TokenType cur = m_lexer.peek().type;\n        return std::find(list.begin(), list.end(), cur) != list.end();\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    static bool is_whitelisted_field(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        static constexpr std::array allowed = { \"title\", \"body\", \"tag\", \"author\" };\n        return std::find(allowed.begin(), allowed.end(), field) != allowed.end();\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  SQL Translation\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct SqlFragment\n{\n    std::string              sql;     // WHERE clause without the \"WHERE\" keyword\n    std::vector<std::string> params;  // positional parameters to bind in order\n};\n\nclass SqlTranslator\n{\npublic:\n    // Compile AST into SQL WHERE fragment\n    SqlFragment compile(const QueryNode& root)\n    {\n        m_buffer.clear();\n        m_params.clear();\n later splice this fragment into a larger statement.\n    void generate(const Term& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Phrase& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery_phrase(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const FieldFilter& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        const std::string columnName = map_field_to_column(n.field);\n        m_buffer << columnName << \" @@ \";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Not& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"NOT (\";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const And& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" AND \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Or& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" OR \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    // Helpers stemming, etc.\n        std::string sanitized;\n        sanitized.reserve(term.size());\n        for (char c : term)\n        {\n            if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) || c == '_')\n                sanitized.push_back(static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)));\n        }\n        return sanitized + \":*\"; // prefix search\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery_phrase(const Repository Interface & Mock Implementation                       */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\nclass IUserRoleRepository\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IUserRoleRepository() = default;\n    virtual std::vector<Role> fetchRolesForUser(std::uint32_t userId) = 0;\n};\n\n/*\n * A very small in-memory repository to simulate database calls.\n * Thread-safe and deliberately slow to help verify the cache path.\n */\nclass InMemoryRoleRepository final : public IUserRoleRepository\n{\npublic:\n    InMemoryRoleRepository()\n    {\n        // --- Seed demo roles ----------------------------------------\n        Role author;\n        author.id   = 1;\n        author.name = \"Author\";\n        author.permissions.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(Permission::ReadArticle));\n        author.permissions.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(Permission::WriteArticle));\n        author.permissions.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(Permission::PublishArticle));\n\n        Role admin;\n        admin.id   = 2;\n        admin.name = \"Administrator\";\n        admin.permissions.set(); // all bits to 1\n        admin.permissions.reset(static_cast<std::size_t>(Permission::DeleteArticle)); // Example\n\n        m_rolesByUser.emplace(1001U, std::vector<Role>{author});\n        m_rolesByUser.emplace(  42U, std::vector<Role>{author, admin});\n    }\n\n    std::vector<Role> fetchRolesForUser(std::uint32_t userId) override\n    {\n        // Simulate I/O latency\n        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(25));\n\n        const auto it = m_rolesByUser.find(userId);\n        if (it == m_rolesByUser.end())\n            return {};\n\n        return it->second;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, std::vector<Role>> m_rolesByUser;\n};\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n/* 4. Permission Evaluator Service                                     */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\nclass PermissionEvaluator\n{\npublic:\n    explicit PermissionEvaluator(std::shared_ptr<IUserRoleRepository> repo,\n                                 std::chrono::seconds cacheTtl = std::chrono::minutes(2))\n        : m_repo(std::move(repo)), m_cacheTtl(cacheTtl)\n    {\n        if (!m_repo)\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"IUserRoleRepository cannot be null\");\n\n        // Schedule periodic cache pruning\n        m_cleanerThread = std::thread(&PermissionEvaluator::housekeepingWorker, this);\n    }\n\n    ~PermissionEvaluator()\n    {\n        m_shutdownRequested.store(true);\n        if (m_cleanerThread.joinable())\n            m_cleanerThread.join();\n    }\n\n    PermissionEvaluator(const PermissionEvaluator&)            = delete;\n    PermissionEvaluator& operator=(const PermissionEvaluator&) = delete;\n\n    /* Evaluate whether the user has `perm`. Fast path hits the cache. */\n    bool hasPermission(std::uint32_t userId, Permission perm)\n    {\n        const CacheKey key{userId, perm};\n\n        // ---------- 1. Try cache hit (shared lock) -----------------\n        {\n            std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> rLock(m_cacheMutex);\n            auto it = m_cache.find(key);\n            if (it != m_cache.end() && !isExpired(it->second))\n            {\n                return it->second.granted;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // ---------- 2. Slow path ‑ fetch from repository -----------\n        bool granted = false;\n        try\n        {\n            // Launch fetch asynchronously to prevent blocking for too long\n            std::future<std::vector<Role>> fut =\n                std::async(std::launch::async, &IUserRoleRepository::fetchRolesForUser, m_repo, userId);\n\n            const std::vector<Role> roles = fut.get();\n            granted                       = evaluateRoles(roles, perm);\n        }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            // Log & fall back to conservative denial; in real code route to logger\n            std::cerr << \"[RBAC] Repository access failed: \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n            granted = false;\n        }\n\n        // ---------- 3. Store in cache (unique lock) ----------------\n        {\n            std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wLock(m_cacheMutex);\n            m_cache[key] = CacheEntry{granted, std::chrono::steady_clock::now()};\n        }\n\n        return granted;\n    }\n\n    /* Explicitly invalidate all cached permissions for a user. */\n    void invalidateUser(std::uint32_t userId)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wLock(m_cacheMutex);\n        for (auto it = m_cache.begin(); it != m_cache.end();)\n        {\n            if (it->first.userId == userId)\n                it = m_cache.erase(it);\n            else\n                ++it;\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    /* --------------------- Internal Structures ------------------- */\n\n    struct CacheKey\n    {\n        std::uint32_t userId;\n        Permission    permission;\n\n        bool operator==(const CacheKey& other) const noexcept\n        {\n            return userId == other.userId && permission == other.permission;\n        }\n    };\n\n    struct CacheKeyHasher\n    {\n        std::size_t operator()(const CacheKey& k) const noexcept\n        {\n            return (static_cast<std::size_t>(k.userId) << 8) ^\n                   static_cast<std::size_t>(k.permission);\n        }\n    };\n\n    struct CacheEntry\n    {\n        bool granted;\n        std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point ts;\n    };\n\n    /* --------------------- Helper Utilities ---------------------- */\n\n    static bool evaluateRoles(const std::vector<Role>& roles, Permission perm)\n    {\n        const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(perm);\n        return std::any_of(roles.begin(), roles.end(),\n                           [index](const Role& r) { return r.permissions.test(index); });\n    }\n\n    bool isExpired(const CacheEntry& e) const noexcept\n    {\n        return (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - e.ts) > m_cacheTtl;\n    }\n\n    void housekeepingWorker()\n    {\n        using namespace std::chrono_literals;\n        while (!m_shutdownRequested.load())\n        {\n            {\n                std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wLock(m_cacheMutex);\n                for (auto it = m_cache.begin(); it != m_cache.end();)\n                {\n                    if (isExpired(it->second))\n                        it = m_cache.erase(it);\n                    else\n                        ++it;\n                }\n            }\n            std::this_thread::sleep_for(15s);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------- Data Members -------------------------- */\n\n    std::shared_ptr<IUserRoleRepository> m_repo;\n    std::chrono::seconds                 m_cacheTtl;\n\n    std::unordered_map<CacheKey, CacheEntry, CacheKeyHasher> m_cache;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex                                  m_cacheMutex;\n\n    std::thread                     m_cleanerThread;\n    std::atomic_bool                m_shutdownRequested{false};\n};\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n/* 5. Stand-Alone Test Harness                                         */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\n#if defined(ILBS_RBAC_UNIT_TEST)\n/*\n * A quick single-threaded functional check. Compile with:\n *   g++ -std=c++20 -DILBS_RBAC_UNIT_TEST module_39.cpp -lpthread\n */\nint main()\n{\n    using namespace ilbs::rbac;\n\n    auto repo     = std::make_shared<InMemoryRoleRepository>();\n    PermissionEvaluator evaluator(repo, std::chrono::seconds(10));\n\n    const std::uint32_t user = 1001;\n\n    std::cout << \"User \" << user << \" may publish? \"\n              << evaluator.hasPermission(user, Permission::PublishArticle) << '\\n';\n\n    std::cout << \"User \" << user << \" may delete?  \"\n              << evaluator.hasPermission(user, Permission::DeleteArticle) << '\\n';\n\n    // Invalidate and recheck (should force repo fetch again)\n    evaluator.invalidateUser(user);\n\n    std::cout << \"After invalidation: may publish? \"\n              << evaluator.hasPermission(user, Permission::PublishArticle) << '\\n';\n\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n#endif /* ILBS_RBAC_UNIT_TEST */\n\n} // namespace rbac\n} // namespace ilbs\n```","numLines":323,"startLine":1,"totalLines":323}}}

   authenticated{false};\n\n    bool hasRole(Role r) const noexcept\n    {\n        return std::find(roles.begin(), roles.end(), r) != roles.end();\n    }\n};\n\nstruct ResourceDescriptor final\n{\n    std::string ownerId;\n    bool        isPublic  {true};\n    bool        isPremium {false};\n};\n\n// Convenience hashing for cache key\nstruct PermissionKey final\n{\n    std::string userId;\n    std::string resourceId;\n    std::string action; // e.g. \"read\", \"edit\", \"delete\"\n\n    bool operator==(const PermissionKey& rhs) const noexcept\n    {\n        return userId == rhs.userId &&\n               resourceId == rhs.resourceId &&\n               action == rhs.action;\n    }\n};\n\nstruct PermissionKeyHasher\n{\n    std::size_t operator()(const PermissionKey& k) const noexcept\n    {\n        std::hash<std::string> h;\n        return (h(k.userId) ^ (h(k.resourceId) << 1)) ^ (h(k.action) << 2);\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// LRU Cache (thread-safe)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ntemplate <typename Key, typename Value, typename Hasher = std::hash<Key>>\nclass LRUCache\n{\npublic:\n    explicit LRUCache(std::size_t capacity)\n        : m_capacity(capacity)\n    {\n        if (capacity == 0)\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"LRUCache capacity must be > 0\");\n    }\n\n    std::optional<Value> get(const Key& key)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock lock(m_mutex);\n        auto it = m_map.find(key);\n        if (it == m_map.end()) return std::nullopt;\n\n        // Move accessed element to front (most-recent)\n        m_list.splice(m_list.begin(), m_list, it->second);\n        return it->second->second;\n    }\n\n    void put(const Key& key, const Value& value)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock lock(m_mutex);\n\n        auto it = m_map.find(key);\n        if (it != m_map.end())\n        {\n            // Override existing entry\n            it->second->second = value;\n            m_list.splice(m_list.begin(), m_list, it->second);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Insert new entry\n        m_list.emplace_front(key, value);\n        m_map[key] = m_list.begin();\n\n        // Evict if needed\n        if (m_map.size() > m_capacity)\n        {\n            auto last = m_list.end();\n            --last;\n            m_map.erase(last->first);\n            m_list.pop_back();\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    using ListType = std::list<std::pair<Key, Value>>;\n\n    std::size_t                         m_capacity;\n    ListType                            m_list;\n    std::unordered_map<Key,\n                       typename ListType::iterator,\n                       Hasher>          m_map;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex           m_mutex;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Voter Interfaces\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nclass IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IAccessVoter() = default;\n\n    /*\n     * Return true if this voter grants permission for the action on\n     * the resource by the given user. A voter that is not applicable\n     * should return std::nullopt.\n     */\n    virtual std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext&       user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor res,\n         std::string_view         action) const = 0;\n\nprotected:\n    // helper for inheritors\n    static bool roleIn(const UserContext& user, Role r)\n    {\n        return user.hasRole(r);\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Concrete Voters\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// 1. Role-based voter --------------------------------------------------------\nclass RoleVoter final : public IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor /*res*/,\n         std::string_view action) const override\n    {\n        if (action == \"admin\")\n        {\n            // Only admins may perform \"admin\" actions\n            return roleIn(user, Role::Admin) || roleIn(user, Role::SuperAdmin);\n        }\n\n        // Non-admin actions are not this voter's responsibility\n        return std::nullopt;\n    }\n};\n\n// 2. Ownership voter ---------------------------------------------------------\nclass OwnershipVoter final : public IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor res,\n         std::string_view action) const override\n    {\n        if (action == \"edit\" || action == \"delete\")\n        {\n            return res.ownerId == user.id || roleIn(user, Role::Editor) || roleIn(user, Role::SuperAdmin);\n        }\n\n        return std::nullopt;\n    }\n};\n\n// 3. Visibility / subscription voter ----------------------------------------\nclass VisibilityVoter final : public IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor res,\n         std::string_view action) const override\n    {\n        if (action != \"read\")\n            return std::nullopt;\n\n        if (res.isPublic) return true;\n        if (!user.authenticated) return false;\n\n        // Pretend a repository call that certifies premium subscription.\n        // We replace it with simple role check for demo purposes.\n        return res.isPremium ? roleIn(user, Role::Reader) || roleIn(user, Role::Author) ||\n                                   roleIn(user, Role::Editor) || roleIn(user, Role::Admin) ||\n                                   roleIn(user, Role::SuperAdmin)\n                             : true;\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Permission Service (Affirmative-Based Decision Manager)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nclass PermissionService\n{\npublic:\n    // Dependency injection (repositories, config, etc.)\n    struct Dependencies\n    {\n        std::shared_ptr<IUserRepository>        userRepo;\n        std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository>     articleRepo;\n        std::shared_ptr<ISubscriptionRepository> subscriptionRepo;\n    };\n\n    explicit PermissionService(Dependencies deps,\n                               std::size_t    cacheSize = 4'096)\n        : m_deps(std::move(deps))\n        , m_cache(cacheSize)\n    {\n        // Register built-in voters\n        m_voters.emplace_back(std::make_unique<RoleVoter>());\n        m_voters.emplace_back(std::make_unique<OwnershipVoter>());\n        m_voters.emplace_back(std::make_unique<VisibilityVoter>());\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Evaluate permission for a user/action/resource triple.\n     * An \"affirmative\" strategy is used: the first voter that\n     * returns true grants access, while explicit false denies.\n     * If no voter returns a result, deny by default.\n     */\n    bool hasPermission(const UserContext& user,\n                       const ResourceDescriptor& resource,\n                       std::string_view action)\n    {\n        // Check cache\n        PermissionKey key{user.id, resource.ownerId /* acts as resourceId */, std::string(action)};\n        if (auto cached = m_cache.get(key))\n            return *cached;\n\n        bool result = evaluate(user, resource, action);\n\n        // Store in cache\n        m_cache.put(key, result);\n        return result;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    bool evaluate(const UserContext& user,\n                  const ResourceDescriptor& resource,\n                  std::string_view action) const\n    {\n        // SuperAdmin shortcut\n        if (user.hasRole(Role::SuperAdmin))\n            return true;\n\n        // Delegating to voters\n        for (const auto& voter : m_voters)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                std::optional<bool> voteResult = voter->vote(user, resource, action);\n\n                if (!voteResult.has_value())\n                    continue; // abstain ‑> next voter\n\n                if (*voteResult)\n                    return true;  // grant immediately\n\n                // deny overrides all\n                return false;\n            }\n            catch (const std::exception& e)\n            {\n                // Defensive: log & move on to next voter\n                std::cerr << \"[security] Voter exception: \" << e.what() << '\\n';\n            }\n        }\n\n        // No voter granted access, deny by default\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    Dependencies                                     m_deps;\n    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IAccessVoter>>       m_voters;\n    LRUCache<PermissionKey, bool, PermissionKeyHasher> m_cache;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Simple inline unit test (can be excluded in production builds)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#ifdef BLOGSUITE_SECURITY_SELFTEST\n#include <cassert>\n\nstatic void selfTest()\n{\n    PermissionService::Dependencies deps{/*nullptr, nullptr, nullptr*/};\n    PermissionService svc(deps, 32);\n\n    UserContext alice { \"alice\", {Role::Author}, true };\n    UserContext bob   { \"bob\",   {Role::Reader}, true };\n    UserContext admin { \"admin\", {Role::Admin},  true };\n\n    ResourceDescriptor pubArticle  { \"alice\", true,  false };\n    ResourceDescriptor privArticle { \"alice\", false, true  };\n\n    // Public read\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(bob, pubArticle, \"read\") );\n\n    // Private read w/o subscription -> deny\n    assert( !svc.hasPermission(bob, privArticle, \"read\") );\n\n    // Owner can edit\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(alice, pubArticle, \"edit\") );\n\n    // Non-owner cannot delete\n    assert( !svc.hasPermission(bob, pubArticle, \"delete\") );\n\n    // Admin can admin\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(admin, pubArticle, \"admin\") );\n\n    // Cache hit (repeat query)\n    assert( svc.hasPermission(admin, pubArticle, \"admin\") );\n}\n\nstatic int _ = (selfTest(), 0);\n#endif // BLOGSUITE_SECURITY_SELFTEST\n\n} // namespace intraledger::blogsuite::security\n```","numLines":386,"startLine":1,"totalLines":386}}}

        return !user.hasAccess(it.requiredRole);\n                           }),\n            results.items.end());\n\n        return results;\n    }\n\n    // Schedules a complete reindex in the background.  If a reindex has run\n    // recently, the call is ignored to avoid unnecessary load.\n    void scheduleReindexAll()\n    {\n        const auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();\n        const auto last = m_lastReindex.load();\n\n        if (now - last < m_opts.reindexInterval)\n        {\n            spdlog::info(\"Skipping reindex – last run was too recent\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Atomically update so concurrent calls do not queue duplicates\n        if (m_lastReindex.compare_exchange_strong(last, now))\n        {\n            auto self = shared_from_this();\n            m_jobQueue->enqueue(\n                \"search.reindex.posts\",\n                [self]() {\n                    self->reindexAll();\n                });\n\n            spdlog::info(\"Reindex job enqueued\");\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    /*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    /*  Internal helpers                                                    */\n    /*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n\n    void reindexAll()\n    {\n        ScopedMetricTimer tmr(\"PostSearchService::reindexAll\");\n\n        try\n        {\n            auto posts = m_repository->findAllPublished();\n            m_backend->beginBulk();\n\n            for (const auto& post : posts)\n            {\n                SearchDocument doc;\n                doc.id          = std::to_string(post.id());\n                doc.title       = post.title();\n                doc.body        = post.markdown();\n                doc.language    = post.language();\n                doc.tags        = post.tags();\n                doc.requiredRole = post.requiredRole();\n\n                m_backend->index(doc);\n            }\n\n            m_backend->commitBulk();\n            spdlog::info(\"Indexed {} posts into search backend\", posts.size());\n        }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            spdlog::error(\"PostSearchService::reindexAll failed – {}\", ex.what());\n            // Mark reindex time back so we can try again soon\n            m_lastReindex.store(std::chrono::system_clock::now() -\n                                m_opts.reindexInterval);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Records query counts for lightweight popularity analytics.\n    void recordPopularQuery(const std::string& query)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock lock(m_popularMutex);\n        auto& count = m_popularQueries[query];\n        count++;\n\n        if (m_popularQueries.size() > m_opts.popularQueryCapacity)\n        {\n            // Remove least frequently used (simple O(n) pass)\n            auto lfuIt = std::min_element(\n                m_popularQueries.begin(), m_popularQueries.end(),\n                [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.second < b.second; });\n\n            if (lfuIt != m_popularQueries.end())\n                m_popularQueries.erase(lfuIt);\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    /*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    /*  Members                                                             */\n    /*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    std::shared_ptr<repository::IBlogPostRepository> m_repository;\n std::atomic<std::chrono::system_clock::time_point>\n                                                    m_lastReindex;\n\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::size_t>     m_popularQueries;\n    std::shared_mutex                                m_popularMutex;\n};\n\n/*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n/*  Factory function for DI container registration.                         */\n/*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\nstd::shared_ptr<PostSearchService>\nmakePostSearchService(const core::Config& cfg,\n                      std::shared_ptr<repository::IBlogPostRepository> repo,\n                      std::shared_ptr<ISearchBackend>                  backend,\n                      std::shared_ptr<jobs::IJobQueue>                 queue)\n{\n    PostSearchService::Options opts;\n    opts.reindexInterval =\n        std::chrono::seconds(cfg.get<int>(\"search.reindex_interval_sec\", 21600));\n    opts.popularQueryCapacity =\n        cfg.get<std::size_t>(\"search.popular_query_capacity\", 500);\n    opts.defaultLimit = cfg.get<std::size_t>(\"search.default_limit\", 20);\n\n SecureTokenService (Core Token Generation)\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_74.cpp\n// Lines: 146-197\nclass SecureTokenService\n{\npublic:\n    struct Config\n    {\n        std::string       secretKey;     // HMAC secret (binary safe)\n        Seconds           defaultTTL     = Seconds { 900 }; // 15 minutes\n        std::string       issuer         = \"IntraLedger-BlogSuite\";\n        std::size_t       randomBytes    = 16;  // Random nonce length\n    };\n\n    std::string generateToken(Json userPayload,\n                              std::optional<Seconds> ttl = std::nullopt) const\n    {\n        const auto now = Clock::now();\n        const auto exp = now + (ttl ? *ttl : cfg_.defaultTTL);\n\n        userPayload[\"iss\"] = cfg_.issuer;\n        userPayload[\"iat\"] = std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                                 now.time_since_epoch()).count();\n        userPayload[\"exp\"] = std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                                 exp.time_since_epoch()).count();\n        userPayload[\"jti\"] = randomHex(cfg_.randomBytes);\n        // ...\n    }\n\n    Json verifyToken(std::string_view token) const\n    {\n        // Verify HMAC signature, check expiration, return payload\n    }\n};\n```\n\n### module_56.cpp - PermissionService with Voter Pattern\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_52.cpp\n// Lines: 168-338\nclass IAccessVoter\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IAccessVoter() = default;\n    virtual std::optional<bool>\n    vote(const UserContext& user,\n         const ResourceDescriptor res,\n         std::string_view action) const = 0;\n};\n\nclass PermissionService\n{\npublic:\n    struct Dependencies\n    {\n        std::shared_ptr<IUserRepository>        userRepo;\n        std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository>     articleRepo;\n        std::shared_ptr<ISubscriptionRepository> subscriptionRepo;\n    };\n\n    bool hasPermission(const UserContext& user,\n                       const ResourceDescriptor& resource,\n                       std::string_view action);\n};\n```\n\n### module_46.cpp - TokenBucketRateLimiter\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_46.cpp\n// Lines: 132-198\nclass TokenBucketRateLimiter final\n{\npublic:\n    struct Settings\n    {\n        std::size_t maxTokens         { kDefaultCapacity };\n        double      refillRatePerSec  { kDefaultRefillPerSecond };\n        seconds     bucketTTL         { kDefaultBucketTTL };\n        seconds     cleanupInterval   { kDefaultCleanupInterval };\n    };\n\n    bool allow(const std::string& key);\n    std::optional<std::size_t> available(const std::string& key);\n};\n\nnamespace api {\n    bool isLoginAttemptAllowed(const std::string& clientKey);\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Architectural Overview\n\nThe IntraLedger BlogSuite authentication system follows a layered architecture with clear separation of concerns:\n\n1. **Token Layer** (module_74.cpp): Handles cryptographic token generation and validation using JWT-like structures with HMAC-SHA256 signing. This is the core security primitive used throughout the system.\n\n2. **Rate Limiting Layer** (module_33.cpp, module_46.cpp): Two complementary rate limiting mechanisms:\n   - `LoginAttemptThrottler`: Persistent, database-backed brute force protection with per-user and per-IP tracking\n   - `TokenBucketRateLimiter`: In-memory token bucket algorithm for general endpoint protection\n\n3. **Permission Layer** (module_39.cpp, module_52.cpp): RBAC implementation with two distinct patterns:\n   - `PermissionEvaluator`: Permission checking against role-based bitset permissions with caching\n   - `PermissionService`: Voter-based architecture (similar to Spring Security) with pluggable access control voters\n\n4. **Password Management** (module_56.cpp): Complete password reset workflow with secure token generation, rate limiting, and email dispatch integration\n\n### Dependency Graph\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    CORE AUTH MODULES                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  module_74 (SecureTokenService)                                 │\n│      ↑ Used by: Password Reset, OAuth, Session Management       │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_56 (PasswordResetService)                               │\n│      ↑ Depends on: IUserRepository, IEmailDispatcher            │\n│      ↓ Consumes: SecureTokenService patterns                    │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_33 (LoginAttemptThrottler)                              │\n│      ↑ Depends on: orm::EntityManager                           │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_39 (PermissionEvaluator - RBAC)                         │\n│      ↑ Depends on: IUserRoleRepository                          │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_52 (PermissionService - Voters)                         │\n│      ↑ Depends on: IUserRepository, IArticleRepository          │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_46 (TokenBucketRateLimiter)                             │\n│      ↑ Depends on: RuntimeConfig                                │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                              │\n                              ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    DEPENDENT MODULES                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  module_51 (EmailNotificationService)                           │\n│      • Uses: Password Reset notification type                   │\n│      • Depends on: UserRepository for user lookup               │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_24 (PostSearchService)                                  │\n│      • Imports: core/auth/UserContext.hpp                       │\n│      • Uses: RBAC filtering (user.hasAccess(requiredRole))      │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_64 (SearchService)                                      │\n│      • Imports: middleware/UserContext.hpp                      │\n│      • Uses: Role-based result filtering                        │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_66 (ContentMetricsService)                              │\n│      • Uses: UserContext for view deduplication                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Key Interfaces Already Present\n\nThe codebase already demonstrates good interface design:\n\n1. **IUserRepository** - Pluggable permission decision interface\n\n## Decoupling Strategy\n\n### Proposed Architecture: IAuthenticationService Interface\n\nTo extract the authentication system into a standalone SSO service, I propose introducing an abstract interface (`IAuthenticationService`) that follows the **Dependency Inversion Principle**. The main application would depend on this interface, not on concrete implementations.\n\n```cpp\nnamespace intraledger::auth {\n\n/**\n * Core authentication service interface.\n * This abstraction allows the BlogSuite to work with either:\n *   1. The embedded local implementation (current modules)\n *   2. A remote SSO service via HTTP/gRPC adapter\n */\nclass IAuthenticationService\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IAuthenticationService() = default;\n\n    // ===== Token Management =====\n\n    /**\n     * Generate an authentication token for a validated user.\n     * @param userId Unique user identifier\n     * @param roles User's assigned roles\n     * @param ttl Optional token lifetime\n     * @return Signed JWT-like token string\n     */\n    virtual std::string generateToken(\n        const std::string& userId,\n        const std::vector<std::string>& roles,\n        std::optional<std::chrono::seconds> ttl = std::nullopt) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Verify and decode a token.\n     * @param token The token string to validate\n     * @return Decoded claims on success\n     * @throws TokenExpired, TokenInvalid\n     */\n    virtual AuthClaims verifyToken(std::string_view token) = 0;\n\n    // ===== User Authentication =====\n\n    /**\n     * Authenticate user with credentials.\n     * @param email User email\n     * @param password Plain-text password\n     * @return AuthResult containing token and user info\n     * @throws AuthenticationFailed, AccountLocked\n     */\n    virtual AuthResult authenticate(\n        std::string_view email,\n        std::string_view password) = 0;\n\n    // ===== Password Management =====\n\n    /**\n     * Initiate password reset flow.\n     * @param email User's registered email\n     * @throws RateLimitExceeded\n     */\n    virtual void requestPasswordReset(std::string_view email) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Complete password reset with token.\n     * @param token Reset token from email\n     * @param newPassword New password (plain-text, will be hashed)\n     * @throws TokenInvalid, TokenExpired\n     */\n    virtual void resetPassword(\n        std::string_view token,\n        std::string_view newPassword) = 0;\n\n    // ===== Permission Checking =====\n\n    /**\n     * Check if user has permission for an action on a resource.\n     * @param userId User identifier\n     * @param action Action being performed (e.g., \"edit\", \"delete\")\n     * @param resourceId Target resource identifier\n     * @return true if permitted\n     */\n    virtual bool hasPermission(\n        const std::string& userId,\n        std::string_view action,\n        const std::string& resourceId) = 0;\n\n    // ===== Rate Limiting =====\n\n    /**\n     * Check if a login attempt is allowed (not rate limited).\n     * @param identifier IP address or user identifier\n     * @return true if attempt should proceed\n     */\n    virtual bool isLoginAttemptAllowed(const std::string& identifier) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Record a failed authentication attempt.\n     */\n    virtual void recordLoginFailure(\n        const std::optional<std::string>& userId,\n        const std::string& ipAddress) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Record a successful authentication (clears rate limits).\n     */\n    virtual void recordLoginSuccess(\n        const std::string& userId,\n        const std::string& ipAddress) = 0;\n};\n\n// Supporting data structures\nstruct AuthClaims\n{\n    std::string userId;\n    std::vector<std::string> roles;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point issuedAt;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point expiresAt;\n    std::string tokenId;  // jti\n};\n\nstruct AuthResult\n{\n    std::string token;\n    AuthClaims claims;\n    std::string refreshToken;  // Optional for token refresh flow\n};\n\n} // namespace intraledger::auth\n```\n\n### Implementation Strategy\n\n#### Phase 1: Create Interface and Local Implementation\n\n1. Define `IAuthenticationService` interface in a shared header\n2. Create `LocalAuthenticationService` that aggregates existing modules:\n   - Wraps `SecureTokenService` (module_74)\n   - Wraps `PasswordResetService` (module_56)\n   - Wraps `LoginAttemptThrottler` (module_33)\n   - Wraps `PermissionEvaluator` (module_39)\n   - Wraps `PermissionService` (module_52)\n\n```cpp\nclass LocalAuthenticationService : public IAuthenticationService\n{\npublic:\n    LocalAuthenticationService(\n        std::shared_ptr<SecureTokenService> tokenService,\n        std::shared_ptr<PasswordResetService> passwordService,\n        std::shared_ptr<LoginAttemptThrottler> throttler,\n        std::shared_ptr<PermissionEvaluator> permissions)\n        : m_tokenService(std::move(tokenService))\n        , m_passwordService(std::move(passwordService))\n        , m_throttler(std::move(throttler))\n        , m_permissions(std::move(permissions))\n    {}\n\n    // Implement interface methods by delegating to wrapped services\n    std::string generateToken(...) override { return m_tokenService->generateToken(...); }\n    AuthClaims verifyToken(...) override { return m_tokenService->verifyToken(...); }\n    // ...\n};\n```\n\n#### Phase 2: Create Remote SSO Adapter\n\nFor future SSO integration, implement a remote adapter:\n\n```cpp\nclass RemoteSSOAdapter : public IAuthenticationService\n{\npublic:\n    RemoteSSOAdapter(\n        std::string ssoBaseUrl,\n        std::string clientId,\n        std::string clientSecret,\n        std::shared_ptr<IHttpClient> httpClient)\n        : m_ssoUrl(std::move(ssoBaseUrl))\n        , m_clientId(std::move(clientId))\n        , m_clientSecret(std::move(clientSecret))\n        , m_http(std::move(httpClient))\n    {}\n\n    std::string generateToken(...) override\n    {\n        // POST /api/v1/tokens to SSO service\n        auto response = m_http->post(m_ssoUrl + \"/api/v1/tokens\", payload);\n        return response.json()[\"token\"];\n    }\n\n    AuthClaims verifyToken(std::string_view token) override\n    {\n        // POST /api/v1/tokens/verify or use local JWT validation\n        // with SSO public key\n    }\n    // ...\n};\n```\n\n#### Phase 3: Update Dependent Modules\n\nModify consumers to use the interface via dependency injection:\n\n```cpp\n// Before (tightly coupled)\nclass PostSearchService\n{\n    std::shared_ptr<PermissionEvaluator> m_permissions;\n};\n\n// After (loosely coupled)\nclass PostSearchService\n{\n    std::shared_ptr<IAuthenticationService> m_auth;\n\n    // Use:\n    if (m_auth->hasPermission(userId, \"read\", articleId)) { ... }\n};\n```\n\n### Benefits of This Approach\n\n1. **Minimal Code Changes**: Existing modules remain intact; we wrap rather than rewrite\n2. **Gradual Migration**: Switch from `LocalAuthenticationService` to `RemoteSSOAdapter` per-service\n3. **Testability**: Easy to mock `IAuthenticationService` for unit tests\n4. **Separation of Concerns**: Clear boundary between auth logic and business logic\n5. **Configuration-Driven**: Swap implementations via DI container configuration\n\n### API Contract for Future SSO Service\n\n```yaml\n# OpenAPI 3.0 specification snippet\npaths:\n  /api/v1/auth/login:\n    post:\n      summary: Authenticate user\n      requestBody:\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              properties:\n                email: { type: string }\n                password: { type: string }\n      responses:\n        200:\n          description: Authentication successful\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthResult'\n\n  /api/v1/auth/tokens/verify:\n    post:\n      summary: Verify token validity\n      requestBody:\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              properties:\n                token: { type: string }\n\n  /api/v1/auth/password-reset/request:\n    post:\n      summary: Request password reset\n\n  /api/v1/auth/password-reset/complete:\n    post:\n      summary: Complete password reset with token\n\n  /api/v1/auth/permissions/check:\n    post:\n      summary: Check user permission\n```\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The authentication system is distributed across six core modules implementing token management, password reset, rate limiting, and RBAC/permission checking. The codebase already exhibits good interface design with abstract base classes.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - **module_74.cpp**: SecureTokenService (JWT-like token generation/validation)\n  - **module_56.cpp**: PasswordResetService (password reset workflow)\n  - **module_33.cpp**: LoginAttemptThrottler (brute force protection)\n  - **module_39.cpp**: PermissionEvaluator (RBAC with caching)\n  - **module_52.cpp**: PermissionService (voter-based access control)\n  - **module_46.cpp**: TokenBucketRateLimiter (rate limiting middleware)\n\n- **Dependent modules**: module_24, module_51, module_64, module_66 consume authentication services for RBAC filtering, notifications, and user context management.\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The recommended decoupling strategy is to introduce an `IAuthenticationService` abstract interface following the **Dependency Inversion Principle**. This allows the BlogSuite to depend on the interface rather than concrete implementations, enabling seamless swapping between a local implementation (wrapping existing modules) and a remote SSO adapter for the future centralized authentication service.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Create the `IAuthenticationService` interface with methods for token management, authentication, password reset, and permission checking\n  2. Implement `LocalAuthenticationService` as a facade over existing modules\n  3. Update dependent modules to use the interface via dependency injection\n  4. Later implement `RemoteSSOAdapter` that communicates with the external SSO service via HTTP/gRPC\n  5. SecureTokenService (Core Token Generation)\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_74.cpp\n// Lines: 146-197\nclass SecureTokenService\n{\npublic:\n    struct Config\n    {\n        std::string       secretKey;     // HMAC secret (binary safe)\n        Seconds           defaultTTL     = Seconds { 900 }; // 15 minutes\n        std::string       issuer         = \"IntraLedger-BlogSuite\";\n        std::size_t       randomBytes    = 16;  // Random nonce length\n    };\n\n    std::string generateToken(Json userPayload,\n                              std::optional<Seconds> ttl = std::nullopt) const\n    {\n        const auto now = Clock::now();\n        const auto exp = now + (ttl ? *ttl : cfg_.defaultTTL);\n\n        userPayload[\"iss\"] = cfg_.issuer;\n        userPayload[\"iat\"] = std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                                 now.time_since_epoch()).count();\n        userPayload[\"exp\"] = std::chrono::duration_cast<Seconds>(\n                                 exp.time_since_epoch()).count();\n        userPayload[\"jti\"] = randomHex(cfg_.randomBytes);\n        // ...\n    }\n\n    Json verifyToken(std::string_view token) const\n    {\n        // Verify HMAC signature, check expiration, return payload\n    }\n};\n```\n\n### module_56.cpp - TokenBucketRateLimiter\n\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_46.cpp\n// Lines: 132-198\nclass TokenBucketRateLimiter final\n{\npublic:\n    struct Settings\n    {\n        std::size_t maxTokens         { kDefaultCapacity };\n        double      refillRatePerSec  { kDefaultRefillPerSecond };\n        seconds     bucketTTL         { kDefaultBucketTTL };\n        seconds     cleanupInterval   { kDefaultCleanupInterval };\n    };\n\n    bool allow(const std::string& key);\n    std::optional<std::size_t> available(const std::string& key);\n};\n\nnamespace api {\n    bool isLoginAttemptAllowed(const std::string& clientKey);\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Architectural Overview\n\nThe IntraLedger BlogSuite authentication system follows a layered architecture with clear separation of concerns:\n\n1. **Token Layer** (module_74.cpp): Handles cryptographic token generation and validation using JWT-like structures with HMAC-SHA256 signing. This is the core security primitive used throughout the system.\n\n2. **Rate Limiting Layer** (module_33.cpp, module_46.cpp): Two complementary rate limiting mechanisms:\n   - `LoginAttemptThrottler`: Persistent, database-backed brute force protection with per-user and per-IP tracking\n   - `TokenBucketRateLimiter`: In-memory token bucket algorithm for general endpoint protection\n\n3. **Permission Layer** (module_39.cpp, module_52.cpp): RBAC implementation with two distinct patterns:\n   - `PermissionEvaluator`: Permission checking against role-based bitset permissions with caching\n   - `PermissionService`: Voter-based architecture (similar to Spring Security) with pluggable access control voters\n\n4. **Password Management** (module_56.cpp): Complete password reset workflow with secure token generation, rate limiting, and email dispatch integration\n\n### Dependency Graph\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    CORE AUTH MODULES                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  module_74 (SecureTokenService)                                 │\n│      ↑ Used by: Password Reset, OAuth, Session Management       │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_56 (PasswordResetService)                               │\n│      ↑ Depends on: IUserRepository, IEmailDispatcher            │\n│      ↓ Consumes: SecureTokenService patterns                    │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_33 (LoginAttemptThrottler)                              │\n│      ↑ Depends on: orm::EntityManager                           │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_39 (PermissionEvaluator - RBAC)                         │\n│      ↑ Depends on: IUserRoleRepository                          │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_52 (PermissionService - Voters)                         │\n│      ↑ Depends on: IUserRepository, IArticleRepository          │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_46 (TokenBucketRateLimiter)                             │\n│      ↑ Depends on: RuntimeConfig                                │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                              │\n                              ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    DEPENDENT MODULES                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  module_51 (EmailNotificationService)                           │\n│      • Uses: Password Reset notification type                   │\n│      • Depends on: UserRepository for user lookup               │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_24 (PostSearchService)                                  │\n│      • Imports: core/auth/UserContext.hpp                       │\n│      • Uses: RBAC filtering (user.hasAccess(requiredRole))      │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_64 (SearchService)                                      │\n│      • Imports: middleware/UserContext.hpp                      │\n│      • Uses: Role-based result filtering                        │\n│                                                                 │\n│  module_66 (ContentMetricsService)                              │\n│      • Uses: UserContext for view deduplication                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Key Interfaces Already Present\n\nThe codebase already demonstrates good interface design:\n\n1. **IUserRepository** -    */\n    virtual bool isLoginAttemptAllowed(const std::string& identifier) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Record a failed authentication attempt.\n     */\n    virtual void recordLoginFailure(\n        const std::optional<std::string>& userId,\n        const std::string& ipAddress) = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * Record a successful authentication (clears rate limits).\n     */\n    virtual void recordLoginSuccess(\n        const std::string& userId,\n        const std::string& ipAddress) = 0;\n};\n\n// Supporting data structures\nstruct AuthClaims\n{\n    std::string userId;\n    std::vector<std::string> roles;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point issuedAt;\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point expiresAt;\n    std::string tokenId;  // jti\n};\n\nstruct AuthResult\n{\n    std::string token;\n    AuthClaims claims;\n    std::string refreshToken;  // Optional for token refresh flow\n};\n\n} // namespace intraledger::auth\n```\n\n### Implementation Strategy\n\n#### Phase 1: Create Interface and Local Implementation\n\n1. Define `IAuthenticationService` interface in a shared header\n2. Create `LocalAuthenticationService` that aggregates existing modules:\n   - Wraps `SecureTokenService` (module_74)\n   - Wraps `PasswordResetService` (module_56)\n   - Wraps `LoginAttemptThrottler` (module_33)\n   - Wraps `PermissionEvaluator` (module_39)\n   - Wraps `PermissionService` (module_52)\n\n```cpp\nclass LocalAuthenticationService : public IAuthenticationService\n{\npublic:\n    LocalAuthenticationService(\n        std::shared_ptr<SecureTokenService> tokenService,\n        std::shared_ptr<PasswordResetService> passwordService,\n        std::shared_ptr<LoginAttemptThrottler> throttler,\n        std::shared_ptr<PermissionEvaluator> permissions)\n        : m_tokenService(std::move(tokenService))\n        , m_passwordService(std::move(passwordService))\n        , m_throttler(std::move(throttler))\n        , m_permissions(std::move(permissions))\n    {}\n\n    // Implement interface methods by delegating to wrapped services\n    std::string generateToken(...) override { return m_tokenService->generateToken(...); }\n    AuthClaims verifyToken(...) override { return m_tokenService->verifyToken(...); }\n    // ...\n};\n```\n\n#### Phase 2: Create Remote SSO Adapter\n\nFor future SSO integration, implement a remote adapter:\n\n```cpp\nclass RemoteSSOAdapter : public IAuthenticationService\n{\npublic:\n    RemoteSSOAdapter(\n        std::string ssoBaseUrl,\n        std::string clientId,\n        std::string clientSecret,\n        std::shared_ptr<IHttpClient> httpClient)\n        : m_ssoUrl(std::move(ssoBaseUrl))\n        , m_clientId(std::move(clientId))\n        , m_clientSecret(std::move(clientSecret))\n        , m_http(std::move(httpClient))\n    {}\n\n    std::string generateToken(...) override\n    {\n        // POST /api/v1/tokens to SSO service\n        auto response = m_http->post(m_ssoUrl + \"/api/v1/tokens\", payload);\n        return response.json()[\"token\"];\n    }\n\n    AuthClaims verifyToken(std::string_view token) override\n    {\n        // POST /api/v1/tokens/verify or use local JWT validation\n        // with SSO public key\n    }\n    // ...\n};\n```\n\n#### Phase 3: Update Dependent Modules\n\nModify consumers to use the interface via dependency injection:\n\n```cpp\n// Before (tightly coupled)\nclass PostSearchService\n{\n    std::shared_ptr<PermissionEvaluator> m_permissions;\n};\n\n// After (loosely coupled)\nclass PostSearchService\n{\n    std::shared_ptr<IAuthenticationService> m_auth;\n\n    // Use:\n    if (m_auth->hasPermission(userId, \"read\", articleId)) { ... }\n};\n```\n\n### Benefits of This Approach\n\n1. **Minimal Code Changes**: Existing modules remain intact; we wrap rather than rewrite\n2. **Gradual Migration**: Switch from `LocalAuthenticationService` to `RemoteSSOAdapter` per-service\n3. **Testability**: Easy to mock `IAuthenticationService` for unit tests\n4. **Separation of Concerns**: Clear boundary between auth logic and business logic\n5. **Configuration-Driven**: Swap implementations via DI container configuration\n\n### API Contract for Future SSO Service\n\n```yaml\n# OpenAPI 3.0 specification snippet\npaths:\n  /api/v1/auth/login:\n    post:\n      summary: Authenticate user\n      requestBody:\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              properties:\n                email: { type: string }\n                password: { type: string }\n      responses:\n        200:\n          description: Authentication successful\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthResult'\n\n  /api/v1/auth/tokens/verify:\n    post:\n      summary: Verify token validity\n      requestBody:\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              properties:\n                token: { type: string }\n\n  /api/v1/auth/password-reset/request:\n    post:\n      summary: Request password reset\n\n  /api/v1/auth/password-reset/complete:\n    post:\n      summary: Complete password reset with token\n\n  /api/v1/auth/permissions/check:\n    post:\n      summary: Check user permission\n```\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The authentication system is distributed across six core modules implementing token management, password reset, rate limiting, and RBAC/permission checking. The codebase already exhibits good interface design with abstract base classes.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - **module_74.cpp**: SecureTokenService (JWT-like token generation/validation)\n  - **module_56.cpp**: PasswordResetService (password reset workflow)\n  - **module_33.cpp**: LoginAttemptThrottler (brute force protection)\n  - **module_39.cpp**: PermissionEvaluator (RBAC with caching)\n  - **module_52.cpp**: PermissionService (voter-based access control)\n  - **module_46.cpp**: TokenBucketRateLimiter (rate limiting middleware)\n\n- **Dependent modules**: module_24, module_51, module_64, module_66 consume authentication services for RBAC filtering, notifications, and user context management.\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The recommended decoupling strategy is to introduce an `IAuthenticationService` abstract interface following the **Dependency Inversion Principle**. This allows the BlogSuite to depend on the interface rather than concrete implementations, enabling seamless swapping between a local implementation (wrapping existing modules) and a remote SSO adapter for the future centralized authentication service.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Create the `IAuthenticationService` interface with methods for token management, authentication, password reset, and permission checking\n  2. Implement `LocalAuthenticationService` as a facade over existing modules\n  3. Update dependent modules to use the interface via dependency injection\n  4. Later implement `RemoteSSOAdapter` that communicates with the external SSO service via HTTP/gRPC\n  5. Use configuration-driven factory to swap implementations without code changes\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
